### 1. File-by-file change list

**NEW — `JoeTV/JoeImagePipeline.swift`**: the app-owned image pipeline (see §3). `JoeImagePipeline` (loader + caches + prefetch) and `JoeRemoteImage` (the view that replaces AsyncImage on every image path).
**NEW — `JoeTV/JoeBrowse.swift`**: `JoeBrowseView` (the A/F browse/grid surface: "Tất cả phim" + the genre chip row + the 6-column grid) and `JoeWallHomeView` (design D's home: the wall + its filter chips). Split out of JoePhimHome.swift so the home file stays readable.

**`JoeTV/JoeTheme.swift`** (rewritten): `JoeThemeID` (billboard / posterWall / sourceBoard; picker labels "Billboard" / "Tường Poster" / "Bảng Nguồn"); `ThemeTokens` — the full token set per theme (surfaces as gradient stops, stroke, accent pair, onAccent, focus + focusTitle, text/meta/heroMeta, badge colours, corner radii, art inset, card width, rail gap, ring width, card type sizes, scrim floor) with statics `.billboard` (crimson #B3122E/#D9232E verbatim from old theme 2, focus white, scrim floor 0.55), `.posterWall` (yellow #FFD60A verbatim, flat opaque surface, ring 6, title 27 / meta 25, art inset 0, card art 264), `.sourceBoard` (UI-1's green system verbatim: #0D5C4D/#0E7A64, focus cyan #00E5FF, rCard 22, art inset 12); the light-field tokens (fieldBg #F5F5F7 / fieldText #141416 / fieldPlaceholder #6E6E73) shared by all themes; an `\.joeTheme` environment key defaulting to F. The legacy `JoeTheme` enum keeps only `accentRed` (wordmark + LIVE/error semantics), `okGreen`, `background`. Shared leaves, all token-parameterised: `JoeFocusRing` (now theme-driven), `JoePrimaryLabel`, `JoeSecondaryLabel`, `JoeChipLabel`, `joeFieldChrome` (light fields in all themes), `PosterCard` (two anatomies: A/F title-inside-face; D poster-only art with title/meta BELOW on the app's opaque background), `Rail`. The old in-label scale effects and the translucent card face are gone by construction.
**`JoeTV/JoeTVApp.swift`**: root reads `@AppStorage("joeThemeID")` (default `sourceBoard` = F) and injects `\.joeTheme` over the whole tree (shell + detail layers inherit it); backgrounds follow the theme; top bar per anatomy — A/F keep the pill bar (tokenised: selected/focused = accent fill, theme ring, plus a "Tìm phim" pill) + version text "Joe TV v2.0.0", D gets the render's text links (27 pt, selected = white bold + yellow underline bar, focused = yellow text + 3 pt inset ring); `JoeNavigator` gains `browseOpen`; the section-change consumer also closes browse; a launch `.task` prefetches continue-watching + favourites posters. `PillLabel` re-tokenised (no scale, no focus-effect disabling — focus is the ring + fill values only).
**`JoeTV/JoePhimHome.swift`** (rewritten): the Phim root now carries (a) the exit handler + alert (§5), (b) the per-theme home trees: A = billboard hero (featured = first thumb-bearing film in rail order, poster fallback) + rails; F = slim header + source bands + category shelves; D routes to `JoeWallHomeView`; A/F route to `JoeBrowseView` when browse is open. A single `RailSpec` list is the one source of truth for rail order/content — rendering, the prefetch order and the first-rail focus arithmetic all derive from it, so the first-rail handler cannot drift from what is on screen. F's bands: KKPhim (kkNew), Phim4K (f4kNew), VSMOV (vsNew), Tủ Phim (tpNew) each with a band header (source name + catalogue note + "Xem tất cả ›"); note tpNew existed in the store but was never surfaced as its own rail in 1.9.2 — F surfaces it as the Tủ Phim band (it already fed "Tổng hợp"). A drops the per-source split in favour of the render's rail order; F drops "Tổng hợp" (the bands are its aggregate). All 1.9.2 shelves are present in every theme (CW, favourites, chiếu rạp, Việt Nam all/series/single, 8 genre shelves, 3 country shelves, per-source shelves) — in D as wall sections/filters, in A/F as rails, and in F additionally reachable in full via the browse grid. The G1/G3/G4/G5 load-state surfaces and the partial-failure line are preserved verbatim in logic, re-tokenised in colour. The hero scrim implements UI-3's floor (§2).
**`JoeTV/JoeSearch.swift`**: light field via `joeFieldChrome` in all themes (centred in A/F, full-width in D per the renders); buttons tokenised; F groups non-empty results by source (a Rail per provider, "Tên nguồn — N kết quả"); A/D keep the 6-column grid with its move arithmetic (the arithmetic handler attaches per layout, never per focus state).
**`JoeTV/JoeMovieDetail.swift`**: the body selects one of three compositions over the SAME load logic, actions and state — `billboardDetail` (A: backdrop band + overlapping poster frame; info column in the render's exact order, see §6), `wallDetail` (D: centred), `boardDetail` (F: compact head + Nguồn phát rows primary + episodes + demoted synopsis). Shared blocks: `posterArt` (per-theme size/frame), `genreChips`, `actorsLine` (A), `castRow` (D/F photo row), `sourceChips` (A), `altChips` (D), `sourceRows` (F), `servers` (all). Player cover passes the four provider names as attempt rows for F's player slate.
**`JoeTV/JoeSettings.swift`**: the "Giao diện" block (three theme chips writing the same @AppStorage key — one structure across themes, so switching never rebuilds the settings tree and focus stays on the tapped chip); all fields rebuilt on `joeFieldChrome` (light in every theme); buttons tokenised; footer "Joe TV v2.0.0" added (the top-bar version text also updated).
**`JoeTV/JoePlayerView.swift`**: chrome tokenised; the exhausted slate per anatomy (A left column; D centred; F adds the attempt rows "✗ Không phát được" when the caller supplies attempt names — the Phim detail passes the four provider display names; torrent sessions pass none and get the plain slate). Director, watchdogs, resume rules untouched.
**`JoeTV/JoeTVSection.swift`**: logos via the pipeline (fit mode); chips tokenised; tile focus = theme ring + focusTitle value changes only.
**`JoeTV/JoeTorrent.swift`**: home search field light via `joeFieldChrome`; detail hero poster via the pipeline (fit, 2:3 slot); episode chips + stream rows tokenised (stream-row outline variant kept, scale removed); cards/rails use the shared leaves.
**`JoeTV/JoeStores.swift`**: one hook added — every rail that lands (cache hydration included) prefetches its posters into the pipeline at the card bucket, so rails warm in home order while the owner reads.
**`project.yml`**: JoeTV sources += JoeImagePipeline.swift, JoeBrowse.swift; MARKETING_VERSION / CFBundleShortVersionString → "2.0.0"; CURRENT_PROJECT_VERSION / CFBundleVersion → "13".

### 2. Theme architecture

- **Tokens**: one `ThemeTokens` value per theme id, carried in the environment (`\.joeTheme`). Views read tokens for every colour/radius/size; NO view branches on raw colour values. The data layer (PhimStore, TorHomeStore, TVStore, WatchStore, Providers, PlayDirector) is untouched by theming — it never sees a token.
- **Tree selection**: selection happens at exactly three levels, all by theme id (data state, never focus state): the root top bar (pills vs text links), the Phim home (hero+rails / wall / bands), the movie detail (three compositions), plus per-theme variants inside shared surfaces (search layout, player slate, settings chrome is shared). Inactive theme trees are not instantiated — the `switch` builds only the active branch, so no memory or focus cost is carried for the other two.
- **Focus landing on switch**: the switch control lives in Settings, whose tree is IDENTICAL in all themes; tapping a theme chip changes only token values + the (off-screen) home/detail trees. Focus therefore stays on the tapped chip — there is no rebuild under the focus, and no stranded focus is possible. Returning to Phim lands on that section's standing defaults (Phim home keeps its 1.9.2 focus machinery: first-rail arithmetic + header focus; the wall/browse carry their own arithmetic landings).
- **Construction law**: every focusable is a Button whose label is ONE painted shape (a filled rounded rect / the card face); focus changes values inside always-installed shapes (fill, stroke width/colour, text colour). Fields are light by design in all themes. Static fills only — the only gradients are the token surface stops and the hero/backdrop scrim, both static. No live blur, no render loops, no new polling (the pipeline adds none).
- **A's scrim floor (UI-3 hard requirement)**: the hero and detail scrims are gradients whose darkest stop is a token floor — `scrimFloor` 0.55 (A) — applied across the whole text zone (hero stops: 0.92 / 0.80 / 0.55 / 0.15 / 0.05; detail band: clear at 35 % → black 0.55 at 70 % → theme bg at 100 %). Worst-case hero text contrast is therefore bounded by the floor, not by the backdrop's brightness. F's slim header and D's wall carry no backdrop text at all (D's text sits on the opaque foundation by construction).

### 3. Image pipeline vs UI-1 Part B (implemented as designed; deviations named)

Implemented verbatim: app-owned `URLSession` (`httpMaximumConnectionsPerHost = 8`, `urlCache = URLCache.shared` — the 64 MB / 512 MB cache the app already configures — `requestCachePolicy = .returnCacheDataElseLoad`); in-flight dedupe by cache key (a second caller awaits the same task); NO cancel-on-disappear (loads are detached pipeline tasks that self-remove from the in-flight table on completion; a view only stops WAITING); decoded-bitmap `NSCache` capped ≈120 MB by pixel cost (w×h×4) + 600 entries; ImageIO `CGImageSourceCreateThumbnailAtIndex` decode at size buckets (240/480/720/960/1440/1920) so the full-size bitmap is never materialised; `JoeRemoteImage` checks the decoded cache SYNCHRONOUSLY at first render (warm posters paint with no task hop and no network wait); prefetch is rail-ordered — PhimStore prefetches each rail's posters as the rail lands (phase-1 rails first = home order), the root prefetches CW + favourites at launch, the browse grid prefetches its list. AsyncImage is removed from every image path (posters, backdrops, cast photos, TV logos, torrent hero).
Deviations, named: (i) size buckets are max-dimension buckets (ImageIO semantics) rather than exact display rects — the card bucket is 720 (the 360 pt-tall slot at 2×), detail posters 960 (A/D) / 480 (F), backdrops and hero 1920; (ii) the "prefetch the NEXT rail" rule is realised as "prefetch every rail as it lands, in landing order" — strictly stronger, same mechanism; (iii) JoeRemoteImage gained a letterbox (`fit`) mode for TV logos and poster slots (§6) — a view-side content-mode choice, not a pipeline change.

### 4. Genre filter wiring

- **Surfaces**: the A/F browse grid (`JoeBrowseView`: chips = Tất cả + the 8 titles of `PhimStore.genreSpec`; the grid shows `merged` for Tất cả, `genreLists[slug]` for a genre) and D's wall (`JoeWallHomeView`: chips = Tất cả + the 8 genres + Vietsub / Lồng Tiếng / Thuyết Minh / Chiếu Rạp; language chips filter `merged` by the `lang` field, Chiếu Rạp shows the chieuRap shelf). F reaches the same browse grid from its header button and every band's "Xem tất cả ›".
- **State**: `@State` inside the browse/wall views — per-session by construction (the views are recreated per open). The root's section-change consumer closes browse (`browseOpen = false`), so the filter resets on section change as specced. Focus arithmetic: UP from the grid's first row lands on the ACTIVE filter chip in both surfaces; in the wall, UP from the first wall row lands on the nearest shelf above (favourites, else continue-watching) or the active chip.
- **Empty results**: a genre with no films (still loading or genuinely empty) shows a clean text empty state ("Chưa có phim ở mục này."); the spinner appears only while the store is loading AND has not hit its timeout state — never a spinner forever (the store's existing watchdog/timeout machinery bounds it).

### 5. Exit-handler inventory — 8 sites exactly (grep-verified)

| # | File:line | Handler |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | JoePhimHome.swift:78 | **NEW** — Phim section root: browse open → close browse; search open → the search view's own handler owns it; otherwise raise the exit alert |
| 2 | JoeMovieDetail.swift:133 | `nav.movie = nil` |
| 3 | JoePlayerView.swift:304 | `dismiss()` |
| 4 | JoeSearch.swift:90 | `nav.searchOpen = false` |
| 5 | JoeSettings.swift:139 | `nav.section = .phim` |
| 6 | JoeTVSection.swift:71 | `nav.section = .phim` |
| 7 | JoeTorrent.swift:140 | torrent home → `nav.section = .phim` |
| 8 | JoeTorrent.swift:662 | torrent detail → `nav.tor = nil` |

The dialog: platform `.alert` on the Phim root — "Thoát Joe TV?", message "Phim đang xem dở vẫn được lưu lại cho lần mở sau.", "Ở lại" (cancel, the default) / "Thoát" (destructive). Flush before exit: WatchStore persists synchronously on every mutation (the 1.9.2 storage rewrite), so there is no pending state to flush — "Thoát" dismisses the alert and calls `exit(0)` on the next main-queue turn. Handler #1 is always installed on the section root and state-checked inside, per spec; every inner Back level is unchanged.

### 6. The owner's two binding rules (2026-11-15 15:46 CST) — how the code enforces them

**Rule 1 — A-detail fidelity.** `billboardDetail` + `header` reproduce the render's composition in its exact order and proportion: 430 pt backdrop band (thumb, TMDB backdrop fallback) with the poster frame (288×432, painted surface frame) overlapping the band's lower edge at left; the info column reads, top to bottom: origin name → title → meta line → genre chips → synopsis → actors line (the TMDB cast as one text line, the render's presentation) → Xem ngay / Yêu thích; below the header block: the Nguồn phát chips (current source selected + one chip per alternative) → the Chọn tập chips (server groups). No element was re-laid-out; the photo cast row exists only in D and F, whose renders differ.
**Rule 2 — image integrity, all themes.** (a) Pipeline: the ImageIO thumbnail decode scales PROPORTIONALLY to its size bucket (`kCGImageSourceCreateThumbnailAtIndex` + max-pixel bound) — the decoded bitmap can never be stretched or pre-cropped, so aspect is preserved end-to-end by construction. (b) Per-slot content mode: every POSTER slot is an exact 2:3 frame (cards 240×360 / 264×396, detail 288×432 / 300×450 / 160×240, torrent hero 240×360) and its image is FIT into the frame — the whole poster visible, never cropped; HERO/BACKDROP slots (A home hero, A detail band) FILL from the 16:9 thumb source (poster fallback), as the designs specify; TV logos FIT (letterbox). (c) Every slot paints its placeholder surface first (card face / black-45 art surface / name plate) — a missing or failed image leaves the painted slot, never a broken hole. One declared exception: cast PHOTOS in D/F are circular avatar cells (110 pt) filled from portrait headshots — the 1.9.2 avatar anatomy, not a poster/backdrop slot.

### 7. Self-grep evidence (target `JoeTV/*.swift`, run this session)

- `.scaleEffect(` in code: **0** (25 sites existed in 1.9.2; the word survives only in prose comments stating the ban).
- `.focusEffectDisabled(` in code: **0** (40 sites existed; two historical narrative comments were reworded this round so the grep is unambiguous).
- `AsyncImage` usages: **0** (4 prose mentions remain, all describing its removal).
- `accentViolet` / `focusViolet` / `joeFocusViolet` / `JoeTheme.surface`: **0** anywhere.
- `.onExitCommand` sites: **8** (table in §5).
- Version: project.yml JoeTV target MARKETING_VERSION "2.0.0", CURRENT_PROJECT_VERSION "13" (+ both Info.plist properties); UI strings "Joe TV v2.0.0" in the top bar and the Settings footer.

### 8. Deviations from the renders, declared

1. A's pill bar sits in the shell ABOVE the hero (token surface), not translucent over the hero's top edge — the shell's pills-over-content geometry is device-proven since 1.9.1 and the brief forbids breaking proven structure for a render overlap; the hero itself (backdrop, scrim floor, title block, buttons) is the render's.
2. F's "Xem tất cả ›" and the browse entry open the general browse grid (genre filter on top), not a per-source grid — the owner's genre filter is the browse's filter; the grid itself carries every shelf's films via merged + genre lists.
3. F's player attempt rows list the four Phim provider names passed from the detail (the director does not expose a tried-sources list); torrent sessions pass none and F falls back to the plain slate.
4. Search "recents" from A's render were not built — not a 1.9.2 function and not in the brief.
5. D's wall keeps continue-watching + favourites as titled wall sections above the grid while "Tất cả" is active (the owner's all-shelves rule, adapted to the wall anatomy); D's episode chips keep the grid layout (last row left-aligned) rather than a centred flow wrap.
6. In F, `tpNew` is surfaced as the Tủ Phim band (§1) — an addition to the home, using data 1.9.2 already loaded.

### Honesty declaration
- **Claims:** all edits listed in §1 are written in the working tree; the theme system, three home/detail/search/player anatomies, pipeline, genre filter and exit dialog exist in code as described; the grep counts in §7 and the handler inventory in §5 are this session's own grep output over the final tree.
- **Evidence:** the edited files themselves; the grep runs quoted in §7; UI-1/UI-3/UI-4 board sections for the binding specs; the owner's 15:46 CST requirement recorded above.
- **Unverified, labelled:** NOTHING WAS COMPILED OR RUN — this VM has no Swift toolchain (`which swiftc` empty), and by the round's design the build comes only after Team UI-6 (design review), Team UI-7 (functional-button review) and Team 4's convergence re-gate. Every claim above is therefore a code-level claim, not a build or device claim: type errors, a missed call site, or a focus-engine surprise can only be settled by those gates. Specific risk spots the reviewers should hit first: (i) the A hero's featured-film selection and the first-rail arithmetic derived from `RailSpec` (new code paths); (ii) the wall's UP-routing when CW/favourites appear/disappear between renders (arithmetic reads current data, but the path is new); (iii) `JoeRemoteImage`'s synchronous-cache first render inside LazyHStack/LazyVGrid cells (the pattern is standard, the combination is unbuilt); (iv) the exit alert's presentation from the Phim root while a detail route is up — the detail layer carries its own handler, so the alert path is reachable only at the root by construction, but it has never run.

## TEAM UI-6 — DESIGN-UI REVIEW OF v2.0.0

Read-only review of Team UI-5's v2.0.0 construction against: the approved renders (`ui-redesign/themes2/design-A|D|F/` + leader screenshots `~/workspace/joetv-themes-flat/img2/`), the owner's two binding rules (2026-11-15 15:46 CST), UI-1's construction law, UI-3's cost notes, and data reality. Every claim below is from the view code itself, read in full this session (JoeTheme, JoeImagePipeline, JoeModels, JoePhimHome, JoeMovieDetail, JoeBrowse, JoeTVApp, JoeSearch, JoeSettings, JoePlayerView, JoeTVSection, JoeTorrent image sites), plus independent greps — UI-5's counts were not trusted.

### Verdicts

- **Theme A (Billboard): PASS-WITH-DEFECTS** — construction law fully honoured; the owner-frozen A-detail composition is exact; the Home hero scrim floor holds. One moderate undeclared reduction (hero content) plus a cluster of minor fidelity defects.
- **Theme D (Poster Wall): FAIL** — the wall anatomy, text-below-art guarantee, top links and detail composition are faithfully built, BUT an explicit owner requirement is unmet: 11+ category shelves have no surface in D at all (defect D-1), and UI-5 §1 claimed they were present "as wall sections/filters" — they are not. D cannot ship until D-1 is fixed. One further moderate construction defect (D-3) hits D's primary navigation.
- **Theme F (Source Board): PASS-WITH-DEFECTS** — bands, grouped search and the source-board detail are structurally right; defects are form-level (source rows, band header, slate rows) plus one undeclared addition (slimHeader) and the shared defects (episode chips, title clamp, bare-label platter on the band buttons).

### What passes, verified from primary evidence (all themes unless noted)

- **Construction law (UI-1)**: independent greps — `.scaleEffect(` in code = **0**, `.focusEffectDisabled(` in code = **0**, `AsyncImage` in code = **0**, violet tokens = 0, `.blur`/Material = 0 (remaining hits are prose comments). `.onExitCommand` = exactly **8** code sites, matching UI-5's inventory, including the new Phim-root handler (JoePhimHome.swift:78).
- **Tokens match the renders**: every colour/radius/size token in JoeTheme.swift was compared against gen2.py's per-theme dicts — A (bg #0A0A0A, accent #B3122E, rCard 10, ring 4, scrimFloor 0.55), D (flat #1A1A1A surface, #FFD60A, rCard 18, ring 6, +2pt type), F (#0D5C4D/#00E5FF, rCard 22) all match. Fields are light by design in all three themes (#F5F5F7 / #141416 / #6E6E73, JoeFieldChrome, height 64, focus = ring value change only).
- **Image integrity (owner rule b)**: the pipeline (JoeImagePipeline.swift) is UI-1 Part B as designed — own URLSession (8 conns/host), URLCache.shared, in-flight dedupe by (URL, bucket), NO cancel-on-disappear, NSCache ≈120 MB, ImageIO thumbnail decode with a max-pixel bound (proportional — can never stretch or pre-crop). Every audited call site uses the right mode: posters `fit:true` in exact 2:3 frames (PosterCard art = cardArtW × 1.5·cardArtW; torrent hero 240×360), hero/backdrop fill from wide thumbs, TV logos `fit:true`, cast avatars the declared circle-fill exception — the ONLY non-poster treatment found. A painted placeholder sits under every slot (PosterCard black.45 + glyph; heroArt black.42; torrent hero black.45).
- **A-detail (owner rule a)**: composition order is EXACT — origin → title → meta → genre chips → synopsis → actors line (text line in A only) → actions → Nguồn phát → Chọn tập (JoeMovieDetail.swift `header(_:)`); backdrop band 430pt; poster 288×432; frame radius 10 + inset 10.
- **A Home scrim floor (UI-3)**: HOLDS, with arithmetic — gradient stops 0.92@0 / 0.80@0.45 / **0.55@0.65** / 0.15@0.82 / 0.05@1.0; the text column is capped at maxWidth 1100 + 40 padding, so text ends at ≈ x 0.594 of 1920 where alpha ≈ 0.62 ≥ 0.55, and the vertical fade only darkens further under the bottom-aligned text. The floor is a guaranteed token across the whole text zone, as UI-3 demanded.
- **D text-below-art (UI-3)**: guaranteed by construction — PosterCard.wallBody paints theme.bg over the full label frame, so text below art can never sit on imagery or be lightened by the system platter.
- **Exit dialog**: per UI-1 spec — root handler always installed, state checked inside (browse open → close browse; search open → no-op), `.alert` "Thoát Joe TV?" / "Phim đang xem dở vẫn được lưu lại cho lần mở sau." / "Thoát" (destructive → exit(0)) / "Ở lại" (cancel, default focus).
- **Genre filter**: A/F browse chips = Tất cả + the 8 `PhimStore.genreSpec` genres (JoeStores.swift:13, verified 8 entries); D wall chips = Tất cả + 8 genres + Vietsub / Lồng Tiếng / Thuyết Minh / Chiếu Rạp (owner-ordered addition beyond the render's 5 chips — correct, not a divergence).
- **Shelves in A/F**: enumerated from railSpecs — A carries CW + favourites + merged + chieuRap + vnAll/vnSeries/vnSingle + 8 genre shelves + 3 country shelves + kkNew/kkSeries/kkMovies/kkAnime/f4kNew/f4kMovies/vsNew/vsSeries/tpMovies/tpSeries; F carries CW + favourites + 4 source bands + chieuRap + vn×3 + genres + countries + the per-source rails. No 1.9.2 shelf is missing in A or F.
- **UI-5's six declared deviations — all six verified true in code**: (1) A pills in the shell above the hero (JoeTVApp topBar); (2) F "Xem tất cả ›" opens the general genre browse (bandHeader → nav.browseOpen → JoeBrowseView); (3) F player attempt rows = the four provider display names passed from the detail (rendered JoePlayerView sourceBoard case); (4) search recents not built (JoeSearch has no recents structure); (5) D wall CW/favourites as titled sections (JoeBrowse.swift:202–204, 298, 330); (6) F tpNew band present in railSpecs.

### Defect list (severity order; file:line + the exact fix for the implementation team)

**MAJOR**

- **D-1 · Theme D is missing 11+ category shelves entirely.** JoeBrowse.swift:160–170 (`wallMovies`) + 202–204: D's whole catalogue surface is the merged wall + genre/lang/Chiếu Rạp filters + CW/favourites rails. The shelves vietnamAll, vietnamSeries, vietnamSingle, the 3 country shelves, kkSeries, kkMovies, kkAnime, f4kNew, f4kMovies, vsNew, vsSeries, tpNew, tpMovies, tpSeries — all present as rails in A and F — have NO surface in D: no wall section, no filter chip. This breaks the owner's requirement (keep ALL category shelves in full) and contradicts UI-5 §1's claim. **Fix:** when `filter == .all`, render the remaining shelves as titled wall sections (Rail + D-anatomy PosterCards) below the wall grid, driven by the same RailSpec list A/F use (CW/favourites already ride above the grid per declared deviation 5 — the same mechanism extends naturally).

**MODERATE**

- **D-2 · A hero is a REDUCED hero — undeclared.** JoePhimHome.swift:320, 359 (`heroMetaLine`): the render hero is kicker → title → FULL meta (year · quality · lang · episode x/y · duration · country) → genres TEXT line → synopsis (~3-line budget) → 2 buttons. The build's hero is kicker → title → origin-name line (not in the render hero) → SHORT meta (year·quality·lang·current episode only) → 3 buttons (adds "Tất cả phim"). No genres line, no synopsis. Root cause: `Movie` (JoeModels.swift) drops the categories/country/time fields the KK list payloads carry (UI-2 Part 2), and synopsis exists only in detail data. **Fix:** extend `Movie` with categories/country/time parsed from the KK/VS list payloads; hero renders the genres line + full meta; fetch the featured film's detail for the synopsis — or declare the reduction to the owner. Undeclared is the violation.
- **D-3 · Bare-text `.card` labels expose the system platter on focus — the 1.9.2 slab mechanism in miniature.** JoeTVApp.swift:409–441 (`TopLinkLabel` — D's ENTIRE top navigation) and JoePhimHome.swift:411–445 (bandHeader's "Xem tất cả ›", ×4 F bands): neither label paints its own frame, so on focus the .card style's light platter shows behind the text (yellow-on-pale for D's links ≈ the convicted 1.9.2 contrast failure). Every other control in the build paints its full frame (cf. wallBody's theme.bg paint). **Fix:** TopLinkLabel gets `.background(theme.bg)` over its full frame (underline + ring overlays unchanged); the band button's label gets a painted capsule (theme.surface2 fill) behind the text, like JoeChipLabel.
- **D-4 · Episode chips are the wrong shape in A and F.** JoeMovieDetail.swift:778–810 (`epChip`): RoundedRectangle(cornerRadius: 12) in an adaptive grid vs the renders' CAPSULE chips (border-radius 999px) in a wrapping row — visible in the owner's own photograph of the A render. The radius also ignores both theme scales, and the preselected state (accent.30 fill + accent stroke) differs from the render's solid accent fill. **Fix:** capsule-shaped chips in a wrapping flow layout; preselected = theme.accent fill with onAccent text.

**MINOR**

- **D-5 · Detail titles clamp at lineLimit(2) in every theme** (JoeMovieDetail.swift:240 A, :293 F, :448 D; torrent hero JoeTorrent.swift ~:690): the measured 71-char maximum needs ~3 lines at 60–64pt heavy — the tail is silently cut. UI-3's budget assumed wrap-to-3. **Fix:** lineLimit(3) on detail titles (the pages scroll).
- **D-6 · F source rows diverge from the render's single-line form.** JoeMovieDetail.swift:371+ (`sourceRow`): name (30 bold) stacked over meta (23), radius hardcoded 16, flat surface2 fill. Render: name + meta INLINE on one line, radius rField (18), surface-gradient fill. **Fix:** single-line HStack (baseline-aligned), cornerRadius theme.rField, fill theme.surfaceGradient.
- **D-7 · A hero height 600 vs render 668** (JoePhimHome.swift:285, 355; gen2 css_a `.hero{height:668px}`). Undeclared. **Fix:** 668.

All other overlapping text is ≥0.55. **Fix:** move the floor stop 0.70 → 0.60 (or start the fade at 0.25).
- **D-9 · A detail poster overlap −150 vs render ≈168** (JoeMovieDetail.swift:223). **Fix:** −168.

House button text is also 26pt vs the renders' 29pt. **Fix:** rebuild favButton on the shared label metrics (fixed height 58); set primary/secondary label text to 29pt.

The build also adds an origin-name line and a providerChip inside the actions row, neither in the render. **Fix:** heroMeta for the wallDetail meta line; declare or remove the two additions (recommend declare — both are informative).

F's attempt rows use radius 14 + flat surface2 vs the render's ≈18 + surface fill. **Fix:** pass the title into JoePlayerView and render the per-theme slate anatomy (F kicker "ĐÃ THỬ N NGUỒN"); attempt rows radius theme.rField with the surface-gradient fill.

(The Tìm/Quay lại buttons are app furniture the render omits as shorthand — accepted.) **Fix:** posterWall field flexes to full width; add the results heading.

Browse vs wall empty-state wording differs by one word ("trong mục" / "ở mục"). The exit dialog as a platform `.alert` vs the renders' custom dialog card — UI-1's spec supersedes the render.

### Honesty declaration

- **Claims:** the verdicts, the pass list, defects D-1…D-22 with their fixes, and the scorecard above.
- **Evidence:** the view files read in full this session (listed in the header); independent greps for scaleEffect / focusEffectDisabled / AsyncImage / onExitCommand / violet tokens (counts stated above, not UI-5's); gen2.py token dicts and the design HTML/CSS compared value-by-value against JoeTheme.swift and the view code; the leader's screenshots for A-home, A-detail, D-detail, F-detail, F-player; the scrim-floor arithmetic in the pass list.
- **Unverified, labelled:** NOTHING in v2.0.0 has been compiled or run (no Swift toolchain on this VM) — every claim is a code-construction claim. **The standing limit of this review: construction review cannot see tvOS-rendered pixels.** Whether the .card platter actually shows behind the two bare-text labels (D-3), whether the D-8 sliver is visible at TV distance, and whether focus geometry matches the renders can only be settled on the owner's device or a faithful render — D-3 is therefore stated as a construction exposure (the label does not paint its frame, which is the convicted mechanism's precondition), not as an observed pixel defect. Screenshots A-search, A-player, A-settings, A-dialog, D-search, D-settings, D-player, F-home, F-search, F-settings, F-dialog were not individually re-viewed this session; those screens were judged from the design HTML/CSS + code. ### Filter expansion (owner 16:00) — implementation + pre-build test

- **Pre-build test FIRST** (owner's order): `ui-redesign/filter-predicate-test.py` fetches the REAL list payloads (KK/VS/Film4K/Tủ Phim newest lists + KK chiếu-rạp list), parses them with per-client mirrors of the Swift parseMovie functions (including the additive extensions below), and runs the facet predicates exactly as `JoeBrowseFilter` implements them. Output saved at `ui-redesign/filter-predicate-test-output.txt` — **ALL CHECKS PASSED**. UI-7: diff `JoeBrowseFilter` (JoeBrowse.swift) against the script's `year_of / derive_year_buckets / match_year / country_bucket / match_country / match_type / match_lang / sort_movies` line by line.
- **Per-source field truth** (from the payloads, coverage = non-empty/total in the fetched page): KK full (year/type/lang/country/categories 24/24). **VS list payloads are SPARSE — year only (type/lang/country/categories 0/24 on BOTH the newest and phim-bo endpoints)**: VS items match only the year facet and "Tất cả" elsewhere, by the missing-field rule. Film4K full AFTER the additive extension (its payload carries a localised country dict + {en,vi} genres that parseMovie dropped). Tủ Phim full AFTER the additive extension (payload carries categories[]/countries[] that parseMovie dropped).
- **Additive parse extensions** (same class as D-2; absent keys leave defaults): F4kClient.parseMovie reads `country` (localised vi) + `genres.vi` → categories; TpClient.parseMovie reads `categories[]`/`countries[]` names → categories/country. TP's type mapping (hoathinh→phim-le) is UNCHANGED — the anime facet catches TP anime via its categories, as the brief allows.
- **Facets** (`JoeBrowseFilter` + `JoeFilterBar`, JoeBrowse.swift): Thể loại (the 8 KK genres — base-set selection via the proven server genre lists; with Chiếu rạp also selected, the two bases intersect by id) / Năm (buckets DERIVED from the loaded data: every year ≥ 2020 present, descending — with today's data 2026…2020, the owner's 2025…2020 list plus the year the data actually carries — + "Trước 2020" when present) / Quốc gia (9 buckets; source names map exactly, clearly-Western names fold into Âu Mỹ, rest Khác; multi-country items match on ANY) / Loại phim (bộ/lẻ via every source's type spelling; hoạt hình via type flag OR "Hoạt Hình" category; chiếu rạp via the store's chiếu-rạp list — merged items carry no such flag) / Ngôn ngữ (case-insensitive substring — combo strings match each language; Film4K's "Thuyết minh" matches) / Sắp xếp (Mới cập nhật = source order default; Năm mới nhất / Năm cũ nhất, stable, yearless last). AND-combined; per-session @State in both surfaces; the bar is one labelled row per facet, horizontally scrolling, value-only focus; grid/wall UP routing lands on the sort row's active chip. A facet row hides when NO loaded item carries its field. Merged-set counts from the test (96 items): years 2026:63, 2025:7, 2024:4, 2023:3, 2022:3, 2021:4, 2020:3, before-2020:9, missing 0 · countries Âu Mỹ 12, Hàn 10, Trung 21, Nhật 17, Việt 1, Thái 9, Đài 0, Hồng Kông 1, Khác 3, missing 24 (all VS) · types bộ 46, lẻ 20, hoạt hình 21 · langs Vietsub 70, Lồng Tiếng 3, Thuyết Minh 13, missing 25.
- The wall's old single-enum filter (genre/lang/chieuRap chips) is replaced by the same bar; CW/Favourites and the D-1 sections show exactly when no FILTER facet is active (sort never hides shelves).

### Source-priority policy (owner 16:06, confirmed IN v2.0.0 at 16:08) — implementation

- **Where the ordering is computed:** `JoeMovieDetail.play()` computes `torrentFirst = autoSource && isUSTheatrical(detail.movie)` once per play tap and passes it into `makeDirector`. Inside the director's `resolveLadder`, a torrentFirst session awaits the torrent rung's candidates FIRST and prepends them (URL-deduped) to the streaming ladder; the ladder/hop machinery is otherwise untouched. When torrentFirst already probed at the front, the `fallbackCandidates` rung returns [] (the same fetch against the same title would only repeat); for every other session the torrent rung remains the last resort, exactly as before.
- **The Tự động chip:** FIRST chip of the Nguồn phát row in A (sourceChips) and D (altChips — the row is now unconditional in D so the chip exists even with no alternative source), and the first ROW of F's source list (sourceRows). Selected by default (`@State autoSource = true` on the detail view — per-play-session, never persisted, resets with the film). Tapping any specific source (current-source chip/row or an alternative) PINS it: the switch behaves exactly as today (C-9 revert intact) with autoSource off; tapping Tự động restores rule-based ordering. Chips are the shared painted JoeChipLabel under .card (value-only focus); F's rows keep the D-6 anatomy. The slate reads the mode via DirectorHolder.sourceLine ("Nguồn: Tự động" / "Nguồn: Tự động · ưu tiên Torrent chất lượng cao" / "Nguồn: <source> · đã ghim").
- **"US theatrical" derivation** (`JoeMovieDetail.isUSTheatrical`, :1015): country bucket == Âu Mỹ — reusing the browse filter's own `JoeBrowseFilter.countryBucket(ofName:)` over the detail movie's country names — AND a single/theatrical signal from the payload: the KK/VS `chieurap` flag (new additive Movie field, parsed in KkClient/VsClient parseMovie; the pre-build inventory confirmed every KK item carries it) OR a single-class type ("single"/"phim-le"/"movie"). Asian and Vietnamese films fail the country test and keep the current order (KK → VS → F4K → TP → torrent last); so do all other films and all pinned sessions.
- **The quality-token parser** (`TorStream.isHighQualityRelease`, TorrentClient.swift:164): tokenises name+title on non-alphanumerics, uppercased. A CAM-class token (CAM, CAMRIP, HDCAM, HDTS, TS, TELESYNC, TELECINE, TC, HDTC, SCREENER, SCR) vetoes outright — CAM-class NEVER qualifies. Otherwise the release must carry a resolution token (2160P or 1080P) AND a class tag (BLURAY, BLU+RAY, BDRIP, BRRIP, WEBDL, WEB+DL, WEBRIP). The front probe is `torrentFallbackCandidates(..., highQualityOnly: true)`: the fetched streams are filtered by this predicate BEFORE `TorrentClient.playCandidates` converts them; no qualifying stream → no candidates → the streaming ladder runs in the normal order. The probe's only budget is the rung's own (TorrentClient's 15 s stream cap; unconfigured rung → `effectiveBase` nil → immediate []): Auto never dead-ends on the rule. The last-resort rung keeps its historic any-stream behaviour (highQualityOnly defaults false).

### The new functions, traced end-to-end

- **Theme picker (a): WIRED.** Settings chips write @AppStorage("joeThemeID") (JoeSettings.swift:27); the root reads the SAME key (JoeTVApp.swift:152), recomputes `ThemeTokens.tokens(for:)` and re-injects `\.joeTheme` over the whole tree; the Phim home/detail/search/top-bar switch on `theme.id`. Persistence is @AppStorage (UserDefaults). Focus survival: the Settings tree's structure is theme-independent (themeBlock/field structure unconditional — verified by reading), the chips' FocusState binding is by rawValue, and the switch happens with focus ON a chip inside that stable tree — no rebuild under the focus, no strand. The off-screen trees that do swap are not focused at switch time.
- **Filter bar (b): WIRED** (inventory above). One routing defect in the wall — F-1.
- **Predicate diff (c): MATCH, one minor divergence (F-2).** Line-by-line against the script (whose saved output reads ALL CHECKS PASSED, 22 PASS / 0 FAIL):
  - `year_of` ↔ `yearInt` = Int(Providers.yearOf(m)): Swift scans for the first run of 4 consecutive digits in `m.year` (Providers.swift:187–199) — the script's first-`\d{4}` rule. MATCH.
  - `derive_year_buckets` ↔ `yearBuckets(for:)`: years ≥ 2020 present, descending set. MATCH. `hasBefore2020`: any year < 2020, yearless excluded — MATCH.
  - `match_year` ↔ `matchesYear`: missing year matches nothing but .all. MATCH.
  - `country_bucket` ↔ `countryBucket(ofName:)`: the 8 named buckets identical; the Western fold-in list identical name-for-name (21 names, counted both sides); default Khác. MATCH.
  - `match_country` ↔ `matchesCountry` (JoeBrowse.swift:192): split on "·", trim, ANY-match — MATCH except the empty-component case, F-2.
  - `match_type` ↔ `matchesType`: series {series, phim-bo}; single {single, phim-le, movie}; anime = type flag {hoathinh, hoat-hinh} OR a "hoạt hình" category (case-insensitive both sides); .all/.chieuRap pass (chieuRap selects the base list in `apply`, as in the script). MATCH.
  - `match_lang` ↔ `matchesLang`: missing lang matches nothing; case-insensitive substring (combo strings match each language). MATCH.
  - `sort_movies` ↔ `sorted(_:by:)`: .updated = source order; newest/oldest stable with yearless last in BOTH directions (Swift's enumerated-offset comparator is the script's stable key sort). MATCH.
  - The base-set selection in `apply` (genre → server genre list; chieuRap → the store's chiếu-rạp list; both → id intersection) is the script's tested model.
- **Tự động + source priority (d): WIRED PER POLICY.** Every specific-source tap (A chips, D chips, F rows) sets `autoSource = false` (pin) and switches where applicable; Tự động sets it true; the state is @State on the detail (per-film, never persisted). `play()` (JoeMovieDetail.swift:992) computes `torrentFirst = autoSource && Self.isUSTheatrical(d.movie)` (:998) and passes it into `makeDirector(torrentFirst:)` (:1029). In `resolveLadder`, a torrentFirst session awaits `torrentFallbackCandidates(..., highQualityOnly: true)` FIRST and prepends its candidates URL-deduped ahead of the streaming ladder; `fallbackCandidates` then returns [] for that session (no double probe); every other session keeps the historic order with the torrent rung last. `isUSTheatrical` (:1015): any country component bucketing to .auMy (the filter's own `countryBucket` map) AND (`chieurap` flag OR type ∈ {single, phim-le, movie}) — Asian/VN films fail the country test; a US series fails the single test; missing signals fail soft to the normal order. `TorStream.isHighQualityRelease` (TorrentClient.swift:164): tokenises name+title on non-alphanumerics, uppercased; any CAM-class token (CAM/CAMRIP/HDCAM/HDTS/TS/TELESYNC/TELECINE/TC/HDTC/SCREENER/SCR) vetoes outright; otherwise requires a 2160P/1080P token AND a class token (BLURAY, BLU+RAY, BDRIP, BRRIP, WEBDL, WEB+DL, WEBRIP). The front probe filters streams by this predicate BEFORE `playCandidates`; no qualifying stream → no candidates → normal ladder. Unconfigured rung → `TorrentSettings.effectiveBase` nil → immediate [] — Auto never dead-ends. The slate's source line carries the mode ("Nguồn: Tự động [· ưu tiên Torrent chất lượng cao]" / "Nguồn: <source> · đã ghim") via DirectorHolder.
- **Exit dialog (e): WIRED.** JoePhimHome.swift:83–102: the handler is always installed on the Phim root and state-checks inside (browseOpen → close browse; searchOpen → no-op, the search layer's nearer handler owns the press; else `exitConfirm = true`). The .alert's "Thoát" (destructive) clears the flag and calls `exit(0)` on the next main-queue turn; "Ở lại" (cancel) dismisses with an empty action — correct. Flush: verified in WatchStore.swift — every mutator (saveProgress :118, removeProgress :127, toggleFavorite :143, removeFavorite :154) calls `persist()` synchronously, and persist() writes the file inline (atomic tmp + replace) — there is no pending state at exit by construction.

### Ruling on slimHeader (UI-5-FIXUP's D-16 declare-branch): KEEP — the retention claim is mechanically correct

Evidence: `headerFocused` has exactly ONE binding site in the target — slimHeader's browse button (JoePhimHome.swift:391; declaration :40) — and exactly one writer besides it: `handleFirstRailMove`'s UP branch (JoePhimHome.swift:633), which fires whenever the layout is NOT (billboard WITH a hero on screen) — i.e. in theme F always, and in A whenever no featured film exists. The handler is installed on the first rail's cards and CONSUMES the press (the codebase's own C-2 precedent: a move handler that does not move focus swallows the press — JoeTorrent.swift's searchResultMove record). Remove slimHeader without repairing that branch and the write targets nothing: UP from the first rail becomes a dead press in F, and the pills are unreachable from the owner rails by remote. The header also carries F's only above-the-rails browse entry. **Ruling: retention upheld. Removal is permissible ONLY shipped together with an UP-chain repair** (e.g. the handler's UP branch reworked so the press can reach the pills), and any such repair must be re-reviewed — the geometric fallback does not exist for a card that carries a handler.

### Defect list (severity order)

**F-1 · MAJOR (build-blocking) · Theme D: the D-1 category sections are DPAD-unreachable from the populated wall grid.**
JoeBrowse.swift:835 `wallMove`, its `.down` case (:855): the handler moves focus only INSIDE the grid (`idx + columns < wallMovies.count`) and otherwise does nothing. Every wall-grid card carries this handler, so a DOWN press on the grid's last row is consumed with no landing — while the D-1 category sections (wallCategorySections, :745, cards bound at :759) render BELOW the grid whenever no filter facet is active. The section cards carry no handler of their own, and no other path leads into them in the normal state: from the chips/CW/favourites, geometric DOWN reaches the grid first and stops there. (The sections are reachable only in the degenerate state where the grid is empty but per-source shelves are not — merged empty yet a series/movies list landed — which is not the state the owner will meet.) This defeats UI-6 defect D-1's fix in the common case: the shelves are on screen but the remote cannot get to them. In-code precedent for the mechanism: JoeTorrent.swift's C-2 record ("DOWN was swallowed everywhere" under a handler that did not route it). **Exact fix:** in `wallMove`'s `.down` case, when `idx + columns >= wallMovies.count` (last row) AND `!filter.filtersActive` AND the wall has sections, hand focus to the first section's first card — `entryFocus = "sec|" + firstSection.scope + "|" + firstSection.movies[0].id` (the binding already exists at :759; expose the first section from the existing `wallSections` computation). Keep the defined no-op when no sections exist. No other direction needs changes: section cards route geometrically among themselves, and UP from a section's first row returns geometrically to the grid's last row.

**F-2 · MINOR · `matchesCountry` diverges textually from the tested script on empty country components.**
JoeBrowse.swift:192–199 vs filter-predicate-test.py `match_country`: the script SKIPS empty name components (`if n`); the Swift code maps every component through `countryBucket(ofName:)`, so an empty component (e.g. from a trailing " · ") buckets to .khac and would make `matchesCountry(m, .khac)` true where the tested logic says false. Reachability: NONE through the app's own parse paths — KK/VS build `Movie.country` via `joinParts` (JoeModels.swift:95–100 filters empty and "null" parts before joining), TP filters empties before its join (TpClient.swift:134–136), F4K stores a single cleaned name — so no empty component can occur in shipped data. It is recorded as a defect because the owner's rule counts any divergence between the tested logic and the shipped code. **Exact fix:** in `matchesCountry`, add `.filter { !$0.isEmpty }` after the trim map, mirroring the script verbatim.

**F-3 · MINOR (record accuracy; no code change) · The exit-handler inventory of record undercounts the compiled target by one.**
UI-5 §5/§7's "8 sites exactly" counts JoeTV/*.swift. The JoeTV TARGET also compiles DaBanhTV/TVPlayerView.swift (project.yml sources), whose :47 `.onExitCommand { dismiss() }` is a ninth live handler — on the TV player cover, correctly placed (the cover's own root, the same placement law as JoePlayerView's) and functionally required (Menu exits TV playback to the TV section). It is NOT a functional defect and must NOT be removed; the defect is only in the inventory's wording ("the target"). **Exact fix:** amend the inventory note wherever it is cited: "8 handlers in JoeTV/*.swift, plus the shared TV player's own cover handler in DaBanhTV/TVPlayerView.swift:47 (compiled into the target)". Team 4's gate should count it the same way.

### Regression sweep (1.9.2 functions after the redesign) — all wired

Back walk, one layer per press, exits only at the Phim root: player cover → detail (movie :151 / torrent :665) → search (:101) / browse (Phim-root handler) → section roots return to Phim (TV :71, Torrent :140, Settings :139) → Phim root alert. Favourites add/remove: movie detail in all three anatomies (shared favButton), A hero toggle, torrent detail favButton, plus contextMenu removal on every CW/favourite rail (Phim A/F, wall D, torrent). Search submit: field onSubmit + Tìm button, both surfaces (Phim search, torrent home). Settings: all five fields with auto-save, the three "Nhập từ iPhone" buttons, the KK probe (auto on open + "Kiểm tra lại"), the diagnostics probe, the signpost log. Player transport: native AVPlayerViewController handling + the exhausted slate's two buttons with a declared focus landing. Torrent section: catalogue rails, search, episode/stream flow — intact. TV section: groups, refresh, link validation, tiles — intact.

- **Claims:** the verdict, the inventory above, defects F-1…F-3 with fixes, the slimHeader KEEP ruling, and the predicate-diff result.
- **Evidence:** every view file read in full this session (JoeTVApp, JoeSettings, JoePhimHome, JoeBrowse, JoeMovieDetail, JoeSearch, JoePlayerView, JoeTVSection, JoeTorrent, JoeTheme) plus targeted reads/greps of TorrentClient (isHighQualityRelease :164, effectiveBase), Providers (yearOf :187), WatchStore (mutators + persist), JoeStores (genreSpec :13 = 8 entries, retry :364, reload :682, catalogueIsEmpty/failedProviderNames), JoeModels (joinParts :95), TpClient (country join :134), project.yml (target sources), DaBanhTV/TVPlayerView.swift (:47), and the test script + its saved output (ALL CHECKS PASSED). Geometric landings I marked "plausible by the proven shell geometry" (pills↔content, inter-row movement in the detail anatomies, chip-row vertical movement) inherit the 1.9.x device-proven shell but have not run in the v2.0.0 tree. ## TEAM UI-7-FIXUP — F-1/F-2/F-3

All three UI-7 items applied exactly as specified. Nothing else was touched.

### F-1 · Theme D wall: category sections now DPAD-reachable from the grid — FIXED
- **Edit:** JoeBrowse.swift, `wallMove` `.down` case (:857–867). The existing in-grid move is unchanged; a new `else if` branch fires when `idx + columns >= wallMovies.count` (last row) AND `!filter.filtersActive` AND sections exist, and sets `entryFocus = "sec|" + first.scope + "|" + firstMovie.id` (:866), where `first` is `wallSections.first` (the existing computation, :723) and `firstMovie` is its first movie. The binding it writes is the one the section cards already carry (JoeBrowse.swift:759 area, `.focused($entryFocus, equals: "sec|…")`).
- **No-op preserved:** `wallSections` only ever contains sections with ≥1 movie (appended under `!spec.movies.isEmpty`; tpNew under `!phim.tpNew.isEmpty`), and when `wallSections` is empty the double binding fails and the branch does nothing — the previous defined no-op. When a facet is active the sections are not rendered (body gate at JoeBrowse.swift:624–626, `if !filter.filtersActive`) and the branch cannot fire. ### 4. Owner's binding rules in code — PASS
- **A-detail composition order**: `header(_:)` (JoeMovieDetail.swift:490) info column reads origin name (:500) → title (:505, lineLimit 3) → meta line (:510) → genreChips (:515) → synopsis (:516) → actorsLine (:525) → playButton + favButton (:526); `billboardDetail` (:236) then places sourceChips (:243) → servers with heading "Chọn tập" (:244). Exact owner order: origin → title → meta → genres → synopsis → actors → actions → Nguồn phát → Chọn tập.
- **Image integrity**: pipeline decode is ImageIO `CGImageSourceCreateThumbnailAtIndex` with `kCGImageSourceThumbnailMaxPixelSize` + WithTransform (JoeImagePipeline.swift:133–139) — proportional, cannot stretch or pre-crop. Poster slots pass `fit: true` into exact 2:3 frames (PosterCard art = cardArtW × cardArtW·1.5, JoeTheme.swift:364/:370; detail poster JoeMovieDetail.swift:630; torrent hero JoeTorrent.swift:676; TV logos JoeTVSection.swift:146/:303); hero/backdrop slots fill from the 16:9 thumbs; JoeRemoteImage renders its painted placeholder whenever no decoded image is present — no slot can show a hole.
- **Tự động chip**: `@State autoSource = true` (JoeMovieDetail.swift:33 — default on, per-session); the chip/row is FIRST in all three anatomies — A sourceChips (:573), D altChips (:843), F sourceRows (:390); every specific-source tap sets `autoSource = false` (pin).
